# Code map

Generated by `toolkit/code_map.py build`; do not edit by hand. `python3 toolkit/code_map.py check` fails when it is out of date.

How to use it: find the file here, run `python3 toolkit/code_map.py find NAME` for the exact line (`path:line kind name`), then read only that range. The map has no line numbers, so it changes only when a file or symbol is added, removed or renamed.

433 source files; 0 without a purpose line (no module docstring or file header).

## `editor/capsule_studio/`

- **`__init__.py`**: CapsuleCreatures Studio: editors for CapsuleCreatures projects.

## `editor/capsule_studio/cli/`

- **`__init__.py`**: Command line over capsule_studio.core; see main.py.
- **`__main__.py`**: `python -m capsule_studio.cli` runs the command line.
- **`ai_cmds.py`**: `capsule-studio battle FILE ai ...` (engine G1, D-26 section 1): the battle's AI profiles (core.ai).

## `editor/capsule_studio/ui/`

- **`__init__.py`**: PySide6 user interface: editor panels (editors/), their host contract (panel.py) and the scene editor window (shell.py).
- **`__main__.py`**: `python -m capsule_studio.ui` starts the scene editor.
- **`app.py`**: Starts the scene editor (all panels, or one with --panel).
   - function: `main`
- **`audio_app.py`**: [S7] Starts the audio editor (a game's audio manifest: songs, instruments, samples, MIDI import, sound effects; engine M4 / D-34).
   - function: `main`

- **`audio_instruments.py`**: [S7] The instrument set editor (engine M4, D-34): instrument sets (a window of 128 voices of a bank, or a set with its own voices), each window's voices, one voice's fields by kind (sampled, fixed-rate, reverse, square with sweep and duty, square, wave, noise, key split, drum set) with its envelope's curve, the key splits of a split voice (a key strip and its runs; select keys and set their entry), the keys of a drum set, and the bank view: the windows sets read from each bank and where they overlap (a program past a short set reads its neighbour's voice).

## `editor/capsule_studio/ui/editors/content/`

- **`__init__.py`**: Content pages of the battle editor (the navigator's Content group): the game's own entries, one module per panel group: type_chart (types and the chart), moves, species, parties, presenters, holders (passives and items), data (everything else as JSON), planned (pages not built yet) and json_edit (the JSON text editor they share).
- **`data.py`**: The Data panel ("Other data"): traits and the other top-level settings of the battle manifest that have no page of their own yet, edited as JSON.
   - class: `DataPanel`
   - method: `DataPanel.__init__, DataPanel._fill_json, DataPanel._section_changed, DataPanel.apply, DataPanel.delete_entry, DataPanel.kind, DataPanel.manifest_doc, DataPanel.new_entry, DataPanel.refresh`
   - constant: `JSON_LISTS, JSON_OBJECTS`
- **`holders.py`**: The Passives / Items panel (§3.1 Content): the battle manifest's passives and items, one tab each (titled in the game's words), and the shop lists (a Shops tab).
   - class: `HolderTab, ItemsTab, PassivesItemsPanel, PassivesTab, ShopsTab`
   - method: `HolderTab.build_form, HolderTab.fill_form, HolderTab.open_logic, HolderTab.set_announce, ItemsTab.add_use, ItemsTab.build_form, ItemsTab.fill_form, ItemsTab.set_field, ItemsTab.set_field_effect, ItemsTab.set_recruit, PassivesItemsPanel.__init__, PassivesItemsPanel.refresh, PassivesItemsPanel.tab, PassivesItemsPanel.tab_title, ShopsTab.__init__, ShopsTab._edit, ShopsTab._fill_items, ShopsTab._shop_chosen, ShopsTab.add_item, ShopsTab.add_shop, ShopsTab.refresh, ShopsTab.remove_item, ShopsTab.remove_shop, ShopsTab.rename_shop`
   - constant: `FLAG_BOXES, NEW_RECRUIT_BONUS, NO_POCKET, USE_TEXT_HEIGHT`
- **`json_edit.py`**: The JSON text editor the Moves panel (effect lists) and the Data panel use: a text box with an Apply button and a parse error line.
   - class: `JsonEdit`
   - function: `json_text`
   - method: `JsonEdit.__init__, JsonEdit.apply, JsonEdit.discard, JsonEdit.set_editable, JsonEdit.set_read_only, JsonEdit.set_value`
- **`messages.py`**: The Messages page (Content > Messages; engine C4, S11): the battle's messages, the engine's and the ones the battle declares itself.
   - class: `MessagesPanel`
   - method: `MessagesPanel.__init__, MessagesPanel._cell_changed, MessagesPanel._edit, MessagesPanel._fill_row, MessagesPanel._fill_selection, MessagesPanel._selection_changed, MessagesPanel.add_message, MessagesPanel.add_optional, MessagesPanel.refresh, MessagesPanel.remove_message, MessagesPanel.rename_message, MessagesPanel.rows, MessagesPanel.set_text`

- **`moves.py`**: The Moves panel: type, category, power, accuracy, priority, target, flags (one box per flag the manifest declares in "move_flags"), critical stage, the base of each move resource (one spin per resource, D-25: e.g. PP) and the cost (one row per resource: a number or a named ruleset formula; a move without its own cost pays the engine's default, shown greyed).
   - class: `MovesPanel`
   - function: `max_accuracy, max_base, max_power, max_priority, targets`
   - method: `MovesPanel._fill_handlers, MovesPanel._fill_resources, MovesPanel._flags_changed, MovesPanel._show_bases, MovesPanel._show_flags, MovesPanel.build_form, MovesPanel.commit_cost, MovesPanel.fill_form, MovesPanel.reset_cost, MovesPanel.set_base, MovesPanel.set_cost`

- **`parties.py`**: The Parties panel: reward base, rank, escape, AI flags, the presenters a party shows, its members and what its intro shows.
   - class: `PartiesPanel`
   - function: `default_rank, max_reward`
   - method: `PartiesPanel._ensure_move_combos, PartiesPanel._fill_intro, PartiesPanel._fill_member, PartiesPanel._member_changed, PartiesPanel._member_chosen, PartiesPanel._moves_value, PartiesPanel._passive_picked, PartiesPanel.add_member, PartiesPanel.attach_presenter, PartiesPanel.build_form, PartiesPanel.detach_presenter, PartiesPanel.fill_form, PartiesPanel.member, PartiesPanel.move_presenter, PartiesPanel.remove_member`
   - constant: `PRESENTER_LIST_FRAME, PRESENTER_ROW_HEIGHT`
- **`planned.py`**: Content pages of the battle navigator that are not built yet (see battle_common.PlannedPanel): each says what it will edit, which work package builds it (D-23 §5) and where the data is edited until then.

## `editor/capsule_studio/ui/editors/logic/`

- **`__init__.py`**: Logic pages of the battle editor (the navigator's Logic group): what the game's battle does (D-23 §3.3), one module per panel group: effects_panel (the effect logic of every owner, with blocks for its block view and catalog for the hook / ref pickers and the .ceffect colours), formulas (the ruleset's formulas), pipelines (the pipelines, points and damage calculator) and planned (pages not built yet).
- **`ai_panel.py`**: The Logic > AI page (engine G1, D-26 section 1): the battle's AI profiles (battle.json "ai_profiles") and the ai_score effect hook a profile's hook rule runs.
   - class: `AiPanel, RuleListBox`
   - method: `AiPanel.__init__, AiPanel._fill_selected, AiPanel._picked, AiPanel.add_profile, AiPanel.migrate_flags, AiPanel.move_profile, AiPanel.names, AiPanel.refresh, AiPanel.remove_profile, AiPanel.rename_profile, AiPanel.run, AiPanel.select, AiPanel.set_default, RuleListBox.__init__, RuleListBox._change, RuleListBox._fill_numbers, RuleListBox._selection_changed, RuleListBox.add_rule, RuleListBox.move_rule, RuleListBox.refresh, RuleListBox.remove_rule, RuleListBox.rows, RuleListBox.set_item, RuleListBox.set_number`
   - constant: `DEFAULT_MARK, INDEX_COLUMN, LIST_TITLES, NOTE, NUMBERS_COLUMN, RULE_COLUMN, RULE_COLUMNS`
- **`bench_panel.py`**: Logic > Bench (D-23 §3.3): battle scenarios (capsule.bench/1, the game's bench/*.json) run through the engine's compiled bench, with core.bench.

- **`catalog.py`**: Widgets of the Effects panel that show the effect language's catalogs (D-23 §3.3), all read from core.logic (which reads the engine's effect compiler): - EffectHighlighter: .ceffect syntax colours for the studio's CodeEditor (ui/editors/script.py), the words from core.logic.words(); - ExpressionEdit: a one-line expression field that completes the word at the cursor (context words, reads, functions, the battle's names); - HookPicker: the hook / move phase / point a handler runs on, with what the catalog says about it (context bindings, value, verdicts, draws, may yield, per_foe); - RefPicker: builds a read of a subject's field ("holder.stat(atk)") from the compiler's ref tables, with the argument picked from the battle's structure (stats and stages, statuses, volatiles, flags, types, roles, points); - FunctionPicker: inserts a call of one of the language's functions (core.logic.function_catalog(): signature and help), with the argument count for a function that takes several (odds(n, m[, by, on]): 2 to 4, the battlers user / target).
   - class: `EffectHighlighter, ExpressionEdit, FunctionPicker, HookPicker, RefPicker`
   - function: `_format, _word_rule, completion_words, describe_hook, describe_point, subject_groups`

- **`planned.py`**: Logic pages of the battle navigator that are not built yet (see battle_common.PlannedPanel): each says what it will edit (D-23 §3.3), which work package builds it (D-23 §5) and where the data is edited until then.

## `editor/capsule_studio/ui/editors/structure/`

- **`__init__.py`**: Structure pages of the battle editor (the navigator's Structure group): what the game's battle is made of (D-23 §3.2), one module per panel group: record (the creature record's roles, party size and move slots), stats (the stats), stages (the stages and their tables), chart (the type chart's tiers, settings and matchup grid), categories (the move categories), flags (move flags and tags), conditions (the condition records), turn (the formats and the turn structure), progression (learning, experience, the generation list), transfer (the record's transfer defaults, a box of the Record page), terms (the game's words), text (its code page) and planned (pages not built yet: none now).
- **`categories.py`**: The Categories panel (D-23 §3.2 "Categories"): the battle manifest's move categories, at most the engine's MAX_CATEGORIES.
   - class: `CategoriesPanel`
   - method: `CategoriesPanel.__init__, CategoriesPanel._cell_changed, CategoriesPanel._damaging_clicked, CategoriesPanel._edit, CategoriesPanel._fill_hits, CategoriesPanel._fill_mode, CategoriesPanel._hit_clicked, CategoriesPanel._hit_picked, CategoriesPanel._mode_picked, CategoriesPanel._selection_changed, CategoriesPanel._stats_picked, CategoriesPanel._type_picked, CategoriesPanel.add_category, CategoriesPanel.move_category, CategoriesPanel.refresh, CategoriesPanel.remove_category, CategoriesPanel.rename_category, CategoriesPanel.rows, CategoriesPanel.set_damage, CategoriesPanel.set_hit, CategoriesPanel.set_hit_category, CategoriesPanel.set_mode, CategoriesPanel.set_type_category`
